## 設置關聯角色 (公共設施網絡工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　dcc07b7f84abe79b98-23

**english**　Set Association Role

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-association-role.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-association-role.html

**parameter**　split_content

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- split_content （可選）
- 指定對容器圖徵進行分割后，是否分割容器的關聯內容。 僅當關聯角色為容器時，才會啟用此參數，并且僅適用于線圖徵。 SPLIT—如果對容器圖徵進行分割，則將分割容器的內容。 如果找到平行內容線圖徵，則也會對內容進行分割，并且每部分將包含在最近的容器圖徵內。 如果內容線不平行，則內容將包含在最近的容器圖徵內。 當內容為非空間交匯點對象時，將會復制該內容，以便每個容器圖徵都具有一個交匯點對象作為內容。 當內容為非空間邊對象時，將會分割該內容，以便每個容器圖徵都具有一個邊對象作為內容。 DO_NOT_SPLIT—如果對容器圖徵進行分割，則將不會分割容器的內容。 如果找到平行內容線圖徵，則內容將包含在容器圖徵的兩個部分中。 如果內容線不平行，則內容將包含在最近的容器圖徵內。 當使用非空間交匯點對象內容時，該內容將包含在較大的容器中。 在使用非空間邊對象內容時，該內容將保留為兩個父容器的內容。 這是默認設置。
- Boolean

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-association-role.html#derived-output

## 設置關聯角色 (公共設施網絡工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 公共設施網絡工具箱
- 管理工具集

**english**　Set Association Role

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-association-role.html

**id**　dcc07b7f84abe79b98-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-association-role.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Set Association Role，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入公共設施網絡 [in_utility_network]、域網絡 [domain_network]、輸入表 [featureclass]、資產組 [assetgroup]、資產類型 [assettype]、角色類型 [association_role_type]、刪除語義 [association_deletion_semantics]、視圖比例 （可選） [view_scale]、分割內容 （可選） [split_content]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/utility-networks/set-association-role.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "in_utility_network", "label_zh": "輸入公共設施網絡", "label_en": "Input Utility Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Utility Network; Utility Network Layer", "description_en": "The utility network that contains the asset type with an association role to set."}, {"parameter": "domain_network", "label_zh": "域網絡", "label_en": "Domain Network", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The domain network that contains the asset type with an association role to set."}, {"parameter": "featureclass", "label_zh": "輸入表", "label_en": "Input Table", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The utility network feature class or table where the association role will be set."}, {"parameter": "assetgroup", "label_zh": "資產組", "label_en": "Asset Group", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The asset group that contains the asset type."}, {"parameter": "assettype", "label_zh": "資產類型", "label_en": "Asset Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The asset type on which the association role will be set."}, {"parameter": "association_role_type", "label_zh": "角色類型", "label_en": "Role Type", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the type of association role that will be assigned to the asset type. Container—The container association role type will be assigned. Features or objects of this role type can contain other features and objects as content. Structure—The structure association role type will be assigned. Features or objects of this role type can have other features and objects attached to them. None—No role type will be assigned. These are features or objects that are neither a container nor a structure but do connect to other structures."}, {"parameter": "association_deletion_semantics", "label_zh": "刪除語義", "label_en": "Deletion Semantics", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the deletion semantics for the network features, which defines how content or attachment network features will be managed when the container or structure is deleted. This applies to both container and structure association roles. Cascade—When the container or structure is deleted, all content or attachment network features will be deleted. Set to none—When the container or structure is deleted, its content or attachment network features will not be deleted but will be removed from the containment or structural attachment association. Restricted—If content or attachment network features exist, an error will be returned when attempting to delete the container or structure. The content or attachment network features must be removed before deleting the container or structure."}, {"parameter": "view_scale", "label_zh": "視圖比例 （可選）", "label_en": "View Scale (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Double", "description_en": "The scale at which containment mode will be entered to edit features participating in the container. For example, setting the view scale to 5 means that when you enter containment mode of the container feature, the scale will be 1:5. Units are based on the utility network units, which are located on the Source tab of the utility network layer properties pane. This parameter does not apply to junction and edge objects."}, {"parameter": "split_content", "label_zh": "分割內容 （可選）", "label_en": "Split Content (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the associated content of a container will be split if the container feature is split. This parameter is only enabled if the association role is Container and is only applicable for line features. Checked—The content of a container will be split if the container feature is split. If a parallel content line feature is found, the content will also be split and each new section will be contained by the closest container feature. If the content line is not parallel, the content will be contained by the container feature that is closest to it. When the content is a point feature class, the content is associated with the larger container feature. When the content is a nonspatial junction object, the content will be duplicated so that each container feature has a junction object as content. When the content is a nonspatial edge object, the content will be split so that each container feature has an edge object as content. Unchecked—The content of a container will not be split if the container feature is split. If a parallel content line feature is found, the content will be contained by both sections of the container feature. If the content line is not parallel, the content will be contained by the container feature that is closest to it. When the content is a point feature class or nonspatial junction object content, the content will be contained by the larger container. When working with nonspatial edge object content, the content will be maintained as content to both parent containers. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.un.SetAssociationRole(in_utility_network, domain_network, featureclass, assetgroup, assettype, association_role_type, association_deletion_semantics, {view_scale}, {split_content})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：in_utility_network、domain_network、featureclass、assetgroup、assettype、association_role_type、association_deletion_semantics、view_scale、split_content。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

**text**　用于確定各個輸入點之上的匯流區域。 集水區是匯集水流的上坡區域。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#summary

## 集水區 (即用型工具) · 用法

**id**　dcc87c23831840ffb2-2

**english**　Watershed

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 即用型工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html

**text**　即用型工具箱中的工具是利用 ArcGIS Online 的托管資料和分析功能的 ArcGIS Online 地理處理服務。
將針對各個輸入點返回集水區圖徵。
輸出集水區圖徵是匯集水流的整個上游區域。 例如，路易斯安那州密西西比河上的輸入點或傾瀉點將創建一個集水區圖徵，其中包括從紐約經蒙大拿州到加拿大西部的區域。
如果傾瀉點未落入特定資料源的范圍內，則將不返回集水區圖徵。
如果未指定點標識欄位，則默認將使用唯一 ID 欄位。
每次請求不得超過 1,000 個輸入點圖徵或 30 分鐘。 如果提供了超過 1,000 個輸入圖徵，則服務將立即返回錯誤并停止處理輸出。 如果運行時間超過 30 分鐘，則服務將終止并返回錯誤。
傾瀉點標識欄位必須為整型或字符串類型。 使用由唯一值構成的欄位非常有用。
此工具將創建一個包含集水區面的輸出圖徵圖層。 面圖徵圖層將包含以下各種欄位： PourPtID—如果指定，則使用提供的點標識欄位中的值。 如果未指定任何值，則對應于輸入點圖徵圖層中的唯一 ID 欄位。 Data Resolution—內部用于生成輸出的預處理資料的分辨率（以原生單位表示）。 Area Square Kilometers—每個集水區的面積（以平方公里為單位）。
默認捕捉距離是指定資料庫中資料的分辨率乘以 5。
捕捉點的可選輸出包含 PointID 欄位。 該欄位包含傾瀉點 ID 欄位中提供的值。 該值用于將輸入傾瀉點與其集水區相關聯。
要提供有關此服務的反饋，請訪問 Hydro 論壇。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#usage

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 捕捉距離 （可選）
- 將用于移動輸入點位置的最大距離。 交互式輸入點和記錄的標尺位置可能與 DEM 中的河流位置未完全對齊。 此參數允許服務將該點移動到具有最大匯集水流面積的附近位置。 默認情況下，捕捉距離的計算方法為源資料的分辨率乘以 5。
- String

## 集水區 (即用型工具) · 參數

**id**　dcc87c23831840ffb2-8

**english**　Watershed

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 即用型工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#parameters

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html

**parameter**　DataSourceResolution

**interface**　dialog

**headers**　
- 標注
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- 資料源分辨率 （可選）
- 指定將用于分析的源資料分辨率。 這些值是用于構建基礎水文資料庫的數字高程模型的空間分辨率近似值。 由于許多高程源以弧秒為單位進行分布，為了更方便理解，我們提供了以米為單位的近似值。 空—將使用基于 3 弧秒的資料源（分辨率大約為 90 米的高程資料）構建的水文源。 最精細—將使用所有可能資料源的每個位置的最佳可用分辨率。 這是默認設置。 10 米—將使用基于 1/3 弧秒的資料源（分辨率大約為 10 米的高程資料）構建的水文源。 30 米—將使用基于 1 弧秒的資料源（分辨率大約為 30 米的高程資料）構建的水文源。 90 米—將使用基于 3 弧秒的資料源（分辨率大約為 90 米的高程資料）構建的水文源。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#parameters

**parameter**　SnapDistance

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- SnapDistance （可選）
- 將用于移動輸入點位置的最大距離。 交互式輸入點和記錄的標尺位置可能與 DEM 中的河流位置未完全對齊。 此參數允許服務將該點移動到具有最大匯集水流面積的附近位置。 默認情況下，捕捉距離的計算方法為源資料的分辨率乘以 5。
- String

- 將用于捕捉距離的線性單位。 Meters—單位為米。 這是默認設置。 Kilometers—單位為千米。 Feet—單位為英尺。 Yards—單位為碼。 Miles—單位為英里。

## 集水區 (即用型工具) · 派生輸出

**id**　dcc87c23831840ffb2-18

**english**　Watershed

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 即用型工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**kind**　參數

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html

**parameter**　DataSourceResolution

**interface**　python

**headers**　
- 名稱
- 說明
- 資料類型

**values**　
- DataSourceResolution （可選）
- 指定將用于分析的源資料分辨率。 這些值是用于構建基礎水文資料庫的數字高程模型的空間分辨率近似值。 由于許多高程源以弧秒為單位進行分布，為了更方便理解，我們提供了以米為單位的近似值。 Blank—將使用基于 3 弧秒的資料源（分辨率大約為 90 米的高程資料）構建的水文源。 FINEST—將使用所有可能資料源的每個位置的最佳可用分辨率。 這是默認設置。 10m—將使用基于 1/3 弧秒的資料源（分辨率大約為 10 米的高程資料）構建的水文源。 30m—將使用基于 1 弧秒的資料源（分辨率大約為 30 米的高程資料）構建的水文源。 90m—將使用基于 3 弧秒的資料源（分辨率大約為 90 米的高程資料）構建的水文源。
- String

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#derived-output

## 集水區 (即用型工具) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 即用型工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**english**　Watershed

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html

**id**　dcc87c23831840ffb2-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Watershed，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。輸入點 [InputPoints]、點標識欄位 （可選） [PointIDField]、捕捉距離 （可選） [SnapDistance]、捕捉距離單位 （可選） [SnapDistanceUnits]、資料源分辨率 （可選） [DataSourceResolution]、概化 （可選） [Generalize]、返回捕捉到的點 （可選） [ReturnSnappedPoints]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#parameters

## 集水區 (即用型工具) · 英文版工具簽章與參數順序

**english**　Watershed

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 即用型工具箱
- 水文分析工具集

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html

**id**　dcc87c23831840ffb2-schema

**kind**　版本參數表

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#parameters

**source_language**　en

**content_version**　3.8

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/tool-reference/ready-to-use/watershed.html#parameters

**parameters**　[{"parameter": "InputPoints", "label_zh": "輸入點", "label_en": "Input Points", "optional_label": false, "datatype": "Feature Set", "description_en": "The point features that will be used for calculating watersheds. These are referred to as pour points, because it is the location where water pours out of the watershed."}, {"parameter": "PointIDField", "label_zh": "點標識欄位 （可選）", "label_en": "Point Identification Field (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "An integer or string field used to identify the input points. The default is to use the unique ID field."}, {"parameter": "SnapDistance", "label_zh": "捕捉距離 （可選）", "label_en": "Snap Distance (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The maximum distance the location of an input point will be moved. Interactive input points and documented gage locations may not align exactly with the stream location in the DEM. This parameter allows the service to move the point to a nearby location with the largest contributing area. By default, the snapping distance is calculated as the resolution of the source data multiplied by 5."}, {"parameter": "SnapDistanceUnits", "label_zh": "捕捉距離單位 （可選）", "label_en": "Snap Distance Units (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "The linear units that will be used for the snap distance. Meters—The units are meters. This is the default. Kilometers—The units are kilometers. Feet—The units are feet. Yards—The units are yards. Miles—The units are miles."}, {"parameter": "DataSourceResolution", "label_zh": "資料源分辨率 （可選）", "label_en": "Data Source Resolution (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "String", "description_en": "Specifies the source data resolution that will be used in the analysis. The values are an approximation of the spatial resolution of the digital elevation model used to build the foundation hydrologic database. Since many elevation sources are distributed in units of arc seconds, an approximation is provided in meters for easier understanding. Blank—The hydrologic source, built from a 3-arc second data source, which is approximately 90-meter resolution elevation data, will be used. Finest—The finest resolution available at each location from all possible data sources will be used. This is the default. 10 meters—The hydrologic source, built from a 1/3 arc second data source, which is approximately 10-meter resolution elevation data, will be used. 30 meters—The hydrologic source, built from a 1-arc second data source, which is approximately 30-meter resolution elevation data, will be used. 90 meters—The hydrologic source, built from a 3-arc second data source, which is approximately 90-meter resolution elevation data, will be used."}, {"parameter": "Generalize", "label_zh": "概化 （可選）", "label_en": "Generalize (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether the output watersheds will be smoothed into simpler shapes or conform to the cell edges of the original DEM. Checked—The polygon boundaries will be smoothed into simpler shapes. Unchecked—The edges of the polygons will conform to the cell edges of the original DEM. This is the default."}, {"parameter": "ReturnSnappedPoints", "label_zh": "返回捕捉到的點 （可選）", "label_en": "Return Snapped Points (Optional)", "optional_label": true, "datatype": "Boolean", "description_en": "Specifies whether a point feature at the watershed's pour point will be returned. If snapping is enabled, this may not be the same as the input point. Checked—A point feature will be returned. Unchecked—No point features will be returned. This is the default."}]

**signatures**　
- arcpy.agolservices.Watershed(InputPoints, {PointIDField}, {SnapDistance}, {SnapDistanceUnits}, {DataSourceResolution}, {Generalize}, {ReturnSnappedPoints})

**text**　依英文版參數表順序列出：InputPoints、PointIDField、SnapDistance、SnapDistanceUnits、DataSourceResolution、Generalize、ReturnSnappedPoints。選填標示、資料型別與英文原說明見 parameters。

## 260018: shapefile 欄位名稱不能以數字結尾。 · 解決方案

**id**　dcdbfb5957a149c97d-2

**english**　260018: The shapefile field names cannot end with numbers.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 260001-260100

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/260001-270000/tool-errors-and-warnings-260001-260025-260018.html#solution

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/260001-270000/tool-errors-and-warnings-260001-260025-260018.html

**text**　重命名以數字字符結尾的解釋欄位名稱，或者通過將輸出保存在文件地理資料庫而不是文件夾中來創建圖徵類而非 shapefile。

## 000109：對于線和點，緩衝距離不能為負。 · 描述

**id**　dce2de55ef86b40d0a-1

**english**　000109: The buffer distance cannot be negative for lines and points.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告
- 101-200

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-00101-00125-000109.html#description

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-00101-00125-000109.html

**text**　當緩衝面圖徵以在面圖徵內部創建緩衝區時，可使用負距離。 線和點圖徵不包含區域，因此這些圖徵類型不能使用負距離。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-00101-00125-000109.html#description

## 000109：對于線和點，緩衝距離不能為負。 · 解決方案

**id**　dce2de55ef86b40d0a-2

**english**　000109: The buffer distance cannot be negative for lines and points.

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 工具錯誤和警告

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/tool-errors-and-warnings/001001-010000/tool-errors-and-warnings-00101-00125-000109.html#solution

**text**　請確認使用的圖徵類是面圖徵類且指定的距離是所需的距離。 如果緩衝線，請考慮線邊參數以使得在創建緩衝區時具有更多選項。

## Apply Building Offsets (Topographic Production Tools) · 匯總

**id**　dcfa220b1dcd8f01c6-1

**english**　Apply Building Offsets

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#summary

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html

**text**　Aligns, moves, and hides building features or calculates offset values for bridge features based on product specification rules defined in an .xml file.
If this tool is run with a building offset XML rule file, it aligns, moves, and hides building marker symbols based on product specification rules defined in an .xml file.
If this tool is run with a bridge offset XML rule file, it calculates the offset values necessary to properly display bridges at a given location. These values can be calculated for bridge features with line or point geometry.
Line bridge feature offsets will be calculated for the width of a bridge based on the overpassing feature that runs through the bridge
Point bridge features offsets include the following: The rotation angle of a bridge based on its overpassing feature The position of the bridge if the original position is not coincident with the overpassing feature

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#summary

## Apply Building Offsets (Topographic Production Tools) · 用法

**id**　dcfa220b1dcd8f01c6-3

**english**　Apply Building Offsets

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#usage

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html

**text**　The feature layers referenced in the rule file must have their symbology defined pursuant to the product specification using ArcGIS Arcade expressions.
The layers must have attribute-driven symbology enabled for marker symbols.
The tool will create an ABO_Rotation field in the Buildings table if one does not already exist and connect the rotation property of building marker symbols to this attribute.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#usage

**text**　ApplyBuildingOffsets example (Python window)
The following Python window script demonstrates how to use the ApplyBuildingOffsets function to align, move, and hide building marker symbols based on the MGCP XML rule file.

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#derived-output

## Apply Building Offsets (Topographic Production Tools) · 代碼示例

**id**　dcfa220b1dcd8f01c6-13

**english**　Apply Building Offsets

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 制圖優化工具集

**kind**　程式或語法

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#derived-output

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html

**text**　# Name: ApplyBuildingOffsets_sample.py
# Description: This script tool aligns, moves, and hides building marker symbols based
# on product specification rules defined in a rule xml file

# Import System Modules
import arcpy

# Setting the environment
arcpy.env.overwriteOutput = 1

# Setting Local Variables
in_map = r'C:\Data\MGCP_TRD_4_4.mapx'
rule_file = r'C:\Data\MTM50_TRD_4_4_BuildingOffsets.xml'

# Calling the Apply Building Offsets tool to align, move, and hide building marker symbols based
# on product specification rules defined in a rule xml file
arcpy.topographic.ApplyBuildingOffsets(in_map, rule_file)

# Getting all messages, warnings, and errors from the tool run and printing the results back to the user
messages = arcpy.GetMessages(0)
warnings = arcpy.GetMessages(1)
errors = arcpy.GetMessages(2)
arcpy.AddMessage('Tool Messages: {}\nTool Warnings: {}\nTool Errors{}\n'.format(messages, warnings, errors))

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#derived-output

## Apply Building Offsets (Topographic Production Tools) · 工具執行與核對

**chapter**　
- 工具參考
- 地理處理工具
- 地形生產工具箱
- 制圖工具集
- 制圖優化工具集

**english**　Apply Building Offsets

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html

**id**　dcfa220b1dcd8f01c6-workflow

**kind**　工具操作

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#parameters

**steps**　
1. 開啟 Analysis > Tools，以英文名稱搜尋 Apply Building Offsets，核對工具箱及工具摘要。
2. 依工具窗格順序核對下列參數；展開同頁的參數卡可查說明、選項與資料型別。Input Map [in_map]、Rule File [rule_file]
3. 依用法、環境與授權段落核對限制。確認輸出位置或就地修改範圍後執行 Run。
4. 在 Geoprocessing History 檢查執行狀態與訊息，再核對結果是否符合使用者指定的範圍與條件。

**provenance**　依官方參數表編排的操作清單

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/tool-reference/topographic-production/apply-building-offsets.html#parameters

如果您能夠使用反饋表單來提供有關導致此錯誤的所用步驟的詳細信息，那么此舉將有助于我們在未來確定和提供解決方案。
幫助信息包括下列內容：
消息 ID 以及錯誤或警告消息
工具的名稱
用于參數的值
相關資料集詳細信息
如果該工具不再處于打開狀態，則您可以在地理處理歷史記錄中找到這些詳細信息。
我們對您所提供的一切信息深表感激。 感謝您為我們改進錯誤代碼文檔所做出的貢獻。
